 h1  a misrepresentation of jill valentine  and how is this jill different from the one capcom has kept hidden in the basement since 2012    h1   re3r is a game with plenty of pros and cons  but i don t want to go over all of that right now  first of all  i don t want to come across as just another armchair critic who dislikes the new version simply because of a dusty layer of nostalgia—no  i want to make it clear right away that i m a new gen fan for whom re3r is only the second game in the entire franchise  after getting to know the franchise better and replaying re3r  i felt quite disappointed by what one of my favorite characters had ultimately become  of course  both characters—jill from the original re3 and jill from re3r—are highly skilled s t a r s  operatives dealing with a viral outbreak  but these two games have completely different narrative tones  they take quite distinct approaches to her personality  emotional state  and interactions   h2  ★ tone and demeanor ★   h2  ✦ original re3 the original jill has a grounded  quietly resilient  and classic action hero stoicism  not surprisingly  this game has significantly more action than the previous two games combined  given the character trends of the time  it s no surprise that jill is portrayed this way  her trauma following the events in the arkley mountains is reflected in her transformation into a more stoic heroine who no longer needs barry burton by her side  this is no longer “jill sandwich”  we re presented with a more seasoned operative who knows how to handle enemies like these  clearly  the game was designed for fans who had already played the previous titles and could therefore see how jill had grown since the events at spencer s mansion  her strength is quiet  professional  and methodical  she isn t prone to outbursts  she focuses entirely on the mission of surviving and escaping  however  despite this more significant change in her personality  we can still recognize the jill that fans have loved since re1  she s kind  caring  compassionate  and willing to do anything to help others who ve gotten into trouble  •  i  “i m sorry about your daughter  but there isn t going to be any rescue  we have to get out of here ”   i  •  i  “hey  is someone wounded back there    calm down  you re safe now  everything is going to be okay ”   i  despite everything  this jill is resilient and has a strong core—no matter what happened  no matter all the obstacles and setbacks  she still continued on her path  never complaining  never losing heart  she knows she has no other choice   i  “this is my last chance for survival    this is my last escape   ”   i  ✦ re3r just like the original jill  the jill from the remake is a product of her time  remake jill is significantly more aggressive  dynamic  and outwardly defiant  she follows trends in modern female protagonists from other games  embodying what s currently quite popular—namely  the archetype of the “strong” female heroine  the so called “badass” with a layer of sarcasm and defiance  leon s  kennedy just rolled over in his grave   this jill is not characterized by stoicism or groundedness  her trauma has taken over her personality  jill s response to this trauma isn t quiet melancholy—it is fierce  raw anger  she uses sharp sarcasm and profanity as armor to mask her vulnerability  which is a fairly common trope for portraying seasoned operatives who ve been through a lot  to many  this approach may seem more realistic  although in my opinion  it s simply more popular  since there are many characters in the re franchise who behave the same way  in my point of view  this jill lacks many of the traits that were shown in the original game  compare her reaction to the wounded mikhail in the original and the remake  •  i  “mikhail     my team was sent here to rescue civilians   coughs  the camera pans down  showing that he is injured   jill  sarcastically   right  how s that going for you ”   i  despite everything  jill still agrees to work with people connected to umbrella in order to save as many civilians as possible  although she supposedly possesses the same kindness and desire to help everyone else  all of this is laced with a thick layer of sarcasm that stands in stark contrast to the original jill  i want to emphasize that this is just my take on it  but i believe that sarcasm is a personality trait that doesn t suit jill in such large doses  we can see similar aspects of her personality even in re1r  but it was toned down and not used in nearly every scene to show just how “badass” this character is  even in the original  there weren t that many cheesy lines  which    well  says a lot   h2  ★ interaction with the nemesis ★   h2  ✦ original re3 nemesis is an overwhelming  terrifying obstacle  jill reacts to it with genuine horror and professional focus  her priority is to find a way past or through it to keep moving  you can tell just how exhausted she was at the end of the game by what she says to nemesis  •  i  “you want s t a r s   i ll give you s t a r s  ”   i  the only cheesy line we get  as if it were a reward  when we defeat nemesis in the final stage  nemesis truly feels like an unstoppable threat—one that has already killed one of your teammates and is now closing in on you unhindered  there s no time to waste on idle chatter  you need to focus solely on your own survival  ✦ re3r nemesis is a personal enemy she actively mocks  jill screams insults at it  turning her fear into pure  defiant spite  nemesis in re3r is a missed opportunity  it s psychologically important how characters view their enemies  how they address them  and exactly how they react  mr  x  re2 re2r  is incredibly intimidating to many players  and none of the main characters would ever dare to say things like “a b tch who can t even swim” or “hey  f ckface ” on the tyrant because it s out of place and ruins the whole unnerving atmosphere  just to clarify  i m not talking about the final lines  i m only referring to how the characters treat their enemies throughout the game   if the main character isn t afraid  why should i be  this jill isn t afraid of nemesis  she s annoyed by his presence and is ready to fight him no matter what   h2  ★ visual design ★   h2  ✦ original re3 a blue tube top  black miniskirt  and a white sweater wrapped around her waist  what a product of late 90s game design  ✦ re3r jill s look has been completely redesigned to prioritize realism and practicality  a layered tactical tank top  utilitarian cargo pants  combat boots  and a shoulder holster   this take might be quite provocative  but i believe that games don t necessarily have to lean toward realism when choosing outfits for their characters  it s just a game  and as far as i m concerned  it doesn t really matter what a character wears  as long as their personality and character development throughout the story are completely captivating  i have to admit that i prefer the original outfit simply because of how iconic and memorable it is  as opposed to the tank top and jeans that have become the go to look for female characters in modern games—as if no other clothing in the world existed  however  i can understand why jill s look was changed—let s be honest  female characters wouldn t be dressed like that today to avoid causing any controversy or conflict    h2  ★ conclusion ★   h2  it s important to remember that this is a remake  and a remake is definitely not a remaster  which has to stay true to everything in the original game  it was obvious that many things would be changed in the name of realism or modern trends  just as they would be in any future capcom remake  however  for me  this will remain a disappointment  given how much my favorite heroine has been altered just to conform to modern standards  still  all that s left is to accept it and welcome the new jill—the gritty  unyielding action thriller protagonist whose coming back to rc   abstract   although extremely short  resident evil 3 remake is a great video game  with satisfying gameplay and an entertaining story   gameplay   this third entry follows the same gameplay cycle as the previous remake  meaning you  the player  have to solve puzzles  get ammo and weapons  and need to fight your way through    there is little innovation between titles  re2   re3   but there are improvements  as seen in players  defensive capabilities  as jill  you are able to sidestep and perfect dodge  meaning you can evade damage  and as carlos  you can also sidestep  but instead of dodging  you parry   the gunplay feels as good as in the previous title  with both characters having distinct loadouts  and although the game is shorter  you will still need to manage ammo  so beware   story   in re3 you play both as jill valentine  a badass super cop  and as carlos  a mercenary    different from re2  these stories generally take place in the same locations and at the same time for the two characters  as these develop a bond of trust and mutual respect throughout the experience   sadly  from what i understood  the story was heavily shrunk down  so maybe give the original a try if you are looking to immerse yourself more in the re franchise   gog normally has good deals    conclusion   being my second resident evil  i was extremely pleased  the story was short  but it was also very interesting  the game felt responsive  and it had some very tense moments that will not make me forget the experience    i recommend resident evil 3  remake  but only if you get it on discount  otherwise it s overpriced for the amount of hours you get

as a bystander and a fan of more action oriented resident evil games  i don t really understand what all the fuss is about  it s one thing to call out the game for it being made in a haste  resulting in a buggy  unplayable mess  and it s another thing to call out a decent game for not answering one s expectations   i played this remake for the first time and… i enjoyed it  i heard people saying that the game is way too short  something like “it lasts 3 hours and that s it ”   that it was rushed  and so on   gameplay wise  it feels like a great stepping stone between resident evil 2 and resident evil 4  we can t quite mow down the zombies as we do in re4  but it isn t really all about running away as it was in resident evil 2  especially the middle part of the story—it s basically a nose peek in the resident evil 4 gameplay  you can actually kill most of the enemies in the game  and you ll end up having a lot of unused ammo in the end  well  that s how i personally played it  re2 was way harder for me in that sense   story wise  it s also decent  it doesn t convey the entirety of re lore  sure  but it s a good game in terms of story to entertain one for a couple of evenings  that is  if you play it carefully and if you are paying attention  if you rush the game  it ll end up being finished in one sitting  the characters  weirdly enough  felt more alive to me than they did in re2  but that s a whole different tale to tell   the ending  spoiler  twist that the vaccine got destroyed just for the sake of upcoming established lore to happen is an underwhelming move  but what can you do about it  right    spoiler   overall  the game does indeed feel like a massive dlc to resident evil 2  the exact same game engine  the exact same graphics  sometimes even the exact same animations  but gameplay  and story wise  it is still a very solid title  compare this case to gta 3 and gta  vice city  the latter being developed in 1 year in a very hasty manner  feeling unfinished and more as a dlc to the former  but it is still considered a timeless classic  the same applies here  it s just an average game that was developed in under a year  it is not supposed to be something bigger than it should be  as i said before—this is merely a stepping stone   certainly  the original version of the game felt way more fleshed out  it had more lore and more cutscenes  the remake retconned some things here and there  but the final product really isn t as bad as people discuss it to be  it is also worth remembering that developing games was a way easier task in some ways than it is nowadays  and the real question is why capcom rushed re3 remake that much  when it could ve gotten the attention it really deserved  in the end  though  i think there is only one completely unconditional reason to hate this game—and it is the price   60 for such a game is way too much to pay on its release date  also  no dlcs  and instead we have to pay for the content that should have been unlocked for us after completing the game   but nowadays  considering all things  it s alright  nothing more  nothing less  especially on sale  it certainly lacks some other game modes as an example  but the game s being this short was actually a good thing for me  growing tired of longer games that require you playing for more than 50 hours to 100  complete them—the more or less relaxing experience that re3 has provided for me was a breath of fresh air  which motivated me to come back to gaming overall 
while i have yet to complete the game  i m writing this review becasue i don t think i want to play any further  allow me to explain   i ll start off with the good  seeing more of racoon city is nice zombie infested streets   jill is still a top notch character  battle hardened after the events of the mansion  remake 3 gives a new breath of life into the character along with a nice redesign that feels different yet familiar   the nemesis is a mixed bag for the game  unlike mr  x   who is continuously on the hunt for leon claire throughout the majority of remake 2   the nemesis encounters are scripted chase scenes  and while they can be intense sequences  the downside is nemesis doesn t have as big of a presence as mr  x did as a result   his redesign is cool  though   now the bad   the game is too linear  unlike the previous game where the map was essentially a series of small mazes that gradually open up into one big maze as you progress  remake 3 s maps are very small  so much so that you can get through them in less than 15 20 minutes  and they re no longer connected to each other either   there are also sudden  random  spikes in difficulty in certain sections   in your first nemesis encounter you will find yourself reloading saves so many times just to try and find your way around the flat faced ♥♥♥♥♥♥  and not in the fun  ah it got me this time  but i ll get passed it in the next round   type of thing  i m talking  oh  you are just piling on obstacle after obstacle in front of me while trying to have me evade your scripted monster that runs like usain bolt     type of difficulty spike   there is an evade system but good luck getting the timing right as  unless you re beyond an arms length away from the enemy  the window of success is about the size of a squirrel s scrotum  and if you do get cought by a zombie there is a very poorly implemented qte that is supposed to let you shake off the zombies  but you still take damage if you succeed it s just less  and in a later stage of the game that little detail will bite you in the arse a lot    they should ve just kept the secondary weapon mechanic where you use whatever knife or explosive you have in your inventory at the time to shake them off  thereby avoiding damage while also damaging the enemy instead   spoilers ahead  this brings me to the main reason why i m most likely done with this game due to the difficulty spike  the random appearance of a horde mode in the final carlos section of the game   everything i brought up about the random difficulty spike culminates in this section  you have to spend an indeterminant amount of time defending a barracaded door in the reception area of a hospital room from an endless horde of zombies  mutated zombies  and hunters  those big  fast  bullet spongey green things from re 1   it is so out of place in an re game and overstays its welcome after the first few minutes   and your tools of defence are the handful of ammo and gear the devs decided to provide you for this section  a dodge mechanic that barely works  and a qte that may as well not be in the game as it does bollock all   it is clearly there to pad out the run time of the game and adds nothing else beyond that  if there s a mod out there that cuts this section out entirely  install that before playing this and save yourself a headache   it s a shame  too  because if it weren t for the random difficulty spikes and the poorly done dodge and qtes  the game can have a lot of fun moments  the nemesis boss fights are pretty fun and intense  the shooting mechanics feel way better in this than they do in remake 2  the game looks amazing graphically and the voice acting is solid all around   if you still want to check it out despite the drawbacks i just mentioned  then wait for think i m done with remake 3

resident evil 3 remake – detailed review  resident evil 3 remake is a visually impressive  action focused reimagining of the original classic  while it succeeds in delivering a cinematic and polished experience  it also makes several design choices that hold it back from reaching the same legendary status as resident evil 2 remake  what s here is good—sometimes very good—but it feels noticeably trimmed down   gameplay   combat  the combat is one of the strongest parts of the game  gunplay feels smooth  responsive  and impactful  especially with the added dodge mechanic that gives jill more mobility and encourages aggressive play  enemies are satisfying to fight  and higher difficulties demand smart positioning and resource management  boss encounters are flashy and intense  though many rely heavily on scripted moments rather than emergent tension   atmosphere   visuals  graphically  the game looks fantastic  raccoon city is brought to life with incredible detail  lighting  and environmental storytelling  the early city sections are easily the highlight  capturing a sense of chaos and collapse that fits the story perfectly  however  the game moves through these locations quickly  rarely allowing players to soak them in or revisit them meaningfully   story   characters  jill valentine is excellent—confident  capable  and well acted  carlos is also a standout  adding personality and depth to the narrative  the story is engaging but feels rushed  with key moments happening back to back without much downtime  this pacing works for action but hurts suspense and emotional weight  players familiar with the original will especially notice missing or shortened sections   nemesis  nemesis is intimidating at first  with a strong introduction that sets high expectations  unfortunately  he quickly becomes more of a scripted set piece than a persistent threat  unlike mr  x in re2 remake  nemesis rarely creates unpredictable pressure  which reduces his long term impact as a horror icon   exploration   puzzles  exploration and puzzle solving take a noticeable back seat  while there are some light puzzles and optional upgrades  the game rarely asks the player to slow down and think  this makes the experience more linear and less replayable from a strategic standpoint compared to other entries in the series   length   replay value  the biggest downside is the game s length  a first playthrough can be completed in a single sitting  and even subsequent runs feel brief  while higher difficulties  challenges  and unlockables add some replay value  they don t fully compensate for how short the main campaign is   pros   stunning visuals and strong atmosphere  smooth  satisfying combat with dodge mechanics  excellent character portrayal  especially jill  high quality sound design and voice acting  fast paced and polished experience  cons   very short campaign  significant cut content from the original  overly linear level design  nemesis is mostly scripted and underutilized  limited puzzles and exploration  final verdict  resident evil 3 remake is a solid action horror game and an enjoyable ride  but it falls short as a remake  it feels more like a streamlined reimagining than a faithful adaptation  if you enjoy action heavy resident evil games like re4 remake or re5  you ll likely have a great time  just don t expect the depth  tension  or longevity of resident evil 2 remake 
coming off of resident evil 2 remake  resident evil 3 feels like a power fantasy  jill valentine is an absolute badass in this game  she moves faster  has a dodge ability  and ammo is far more common than in re2  to provide a movie comparison  re2 is like alien and re3 is like aliens  more explosions  more action  and more firepower  it s a really fun ride from beginning to end  and i would definitely recommend it    just not at full price   40 for this is ridiculous  my casual playthrough took just north of 6 hours to complete  and unlike re2  there is no  second story  with additional or remixed content  while a lot of the re games are fun on re plays  i still don t think that alone is worth  40  however  getting this on sale for  10 is an absolute steal and so worth it    i have to add too that re3 might have my favorite  super weapon to kill the final boss  moment  which looks so awesome in the re engine here  the fight wasn t too hard  but man was it badass 
as an og resident evil fan  this game provides a relatively conflicting experience  it s a competent survival horror game  but a hollow adaptation of the original   i came to this one late  already well aware of the complaints regarding this game and after finally playing it myself  i have to agree with the criticisms    jill is excellent  the gunplay is tight  and it looks great  no issues there  but the missing pieces of content hurt this game  especially when it comes to nemesis  after re2 remake nailed the persistent threat with mr  x  i expected the same organic terror here  instead  nemesis is largely confined to scripted chases and boss arenas  the relentless pressure that defined him in the original just isn t there   even the gore system took a hit  re2 remake let you blow chunks off zombies in gruesome detail  here  that visceral feedback is dialed back noticeably  you can tell this game was rushed out the door  even if capcom did a respectable job with it    newcomers to the franchise won t feel this same disappointment  their main gripe will likely be the shorter campaign and minimal replayability for the price point   that said  grab it on sale  the core experience is enjoyable  and jill valentine deserves more time in the spotlight   any chance to see her back in action is worth it imo

greed    and for this game  the same rings true  what killed any hopes for this game paying tribute to its original  was capcom s need to ride sales hype and push out a new game and make a quick buck   i ve despised this game for years  refusing to touch it with a ten foot pole  but for the sake of fairness  i d finally resolved myself to give it a fair chance  even if it might not change my mind  having experienced it myself  i did somewhat enjoy it  but my overall opinion remains the same   resident evil 3  2020   is a survival horror action adventure game  and the remake of resident evil 3  nemesis  1999   aiming to modernize the final entry in the original re trilogy  and set up a new remake continuity to follow  you re placed during the events of the raccoon city t virus outbreak  set parallel to the story of resident evil 2  as s t a r s member jill valentine attempts to flee the city  all the while pursued by a relentless force bent solely to wipe out her squad  re3r was developed shortly after the release of re2r  to take advantage of the massive buzz that game generated  only it was handed off to capcom s b team  instead of their more experienced a team  ontop of this  they were only given a year of development time to produce the title  resulting in many cut corners  as capcom s final sin  they also decided to package re3 with resistance  a dead by daylight style asym pvp game to increase the price  the original is contested in the community  but has a large enough cult following  and contributed multiple lasting and memorable gameplay systems that justify its existence  dodging  ammo crafting  the live selection system  nemesis  and the stalker enemy type  randomized items  and the iconic mercenaries mode  3r butchers most of these elements   re3r is a fairly linear game  building in some ways off of its predecessor  degenerating in others  much like the shambling horrors you ll be contending with  ammo crafting and resource management make a return  but you re given a handy dodge button that lets you slip past enemies easier if timed properly  similar to revelations 2  though now you enter bullet time and auto aim when perfect dodging   unfortunately  the enemies and some aspects of the game are far less polished  zombie variety is cut down  and the gore is reduced  the map this time around is far more on rails  not giving you many hubs to slowly unwind  in the original  you had a sprawling set of alleys and establishments to go through to solve puzzles and obtain key items  here raccoon city is comprised of two main streets  and the other areas such as the sewers and nest 2 are similarly short  only having maybe two or three major areas  jill doesn t even get to see the rpd one last time  her workplace  instead it is handed off to carlos  the only remotely interesting location this time around is the newly expanded spencer memorial hospital  which i admit is an improvement over what it was in the original  the new enemy types are fun  at least  with the hunter gammas and betas  more engaging than the g adults from re2  requiring a bit more tactical approach  now  we cannot talk about gameplay without addressing the parasite infested elephant in the room  nemesis  in the 1999 game he is scripted  yes  however the frequency of his encounters  as well as the branching path system changing up when he ll appear next  made him less predictable  and a far bigger threat  in re3r  he is given only a handful of limited  fully scripted sequences  relegated largely to boss encounters  making him lose a lot of the impact he once had  his second and final forms in particular being excessive  giving him a bland beast form and a final blob form  birkin g5 called  he wants his fit back   rather than the tentacle armed form and the smaller  but more challenging final mutation from the original  several parts of the original were cut  such as areas  the raccoon city clock tower  the park  the  dead factory  with the aftermath of the skirmish between ubcs and the t 103 s   a boss  gravedigger  a massive earthworm  and mechanics  live choice system and nemesis  encounters   as well as no post game content like mercenaries  the greatly reduced development time likely contributed to this  it s overall content starved compared to the original  it s almost as if even the game isn t proud of itself  as it quickly tries to rush you through every major area and story beat to get to the end as quickly as possible  with none of the new additions lingering long enough to be appreciated  almost like an amusement park ride   the re games were never super big on story quality  but they were fun enough to overlook  3r for the most part does an alright job  but has several brutal stumbles along the way  mostly with jill  boring designs aside  every character in this game has a bad case of sailor mouth  and it doesn t come off as very believable  jill and carlos  in particular  sometimes talk like teenagers in a cod lobby  defusing any tension there could ve been with how corny their one liners can be  and not in a fun way like the past games   it s made worse as this is inconsistent  sometimes giving them genuinely sincere moments  only to be derailed by hilariously uncharacteristic zingers  the car scene  the bridge scene   jill s almost unrecognizeable here compared to any of her other appearances  as you d think s t a r s   resident b e agent and former military bomb disposal specialist would be more level headed  especially after being hardened from the events of re1  but i guess not  none of the other modern games had these problems  not even re8  which had some very bad writing at some points  the only character who acted like this in the past was moira  who is a 20 something with no military background  and gradually improves as a character over the course of the game  it s just a shame to see  resident evil only really sparingly uses crass language when the tone and situation fits  while here it s complete whiplash  ontop of the fact that most of the characters  namely carlos and jill  spend their time calling nemesis playground level insults and dismissing him  washing away any intimidation factor or tension he might ve provided  buildup is also completely ripped out of the game by having nemesis bust through jill s apartment wall only five minutes into the game  as opposed to having you slowly learn that something is chasing you down  not helped by you seemingly being able to stop him with a shelf in the apartment escape setpiece  the creature who just went through a solid brick wall a minute ago   jill is comically aggressive toward nemesis and never scared of him  and every scene has him fail or let her go without reason  no horror game disrespects their main antagonist this badly  it s pathetic  carlos and brad especially are out of tune  brad being nonchalant in the apocalypse and carlos being obnoxiously flirty and nice  taking away their character flaws  for a game and its  fans claiming realism  these characters sure feel like caricatures   all in all  the game itself isn t bad by its lonesome  but compared to the original it s a shameless cashgrab not given enough time to bake in the oven before being haphazardly packaged with a game no one asked for  just to pad its pricetag  it is one of the worst remakes of an existing game i ve ever seen  as it cannot be viewed separately if its  goal was to adapt an existing piece of media  thankfully  you can at least give the original ps1 1999 game a try  even if just on easy mode  on gog or steam  and it s both cheap and comes with an analog control mod nowadays to make battling nemesis easier  i doubt re3 will be revisited for a few decades  so this is the modern version we re stuck with  buy on sale if you must

  full spoilers     i would recommend just playing re2 remake and avoid this game altogether  as has been observed before  this game feels like a glorified dlc for re2 and if you were to play them back to back  this critique would ring true  re2 remake was a tight  suspenseful and challenging horror survival game with great atmosphere  combat mechanics  a good variety of enemies and environments and decent characters who you cared about  for me there is always a distinct sense of anxiety hanging over you as you carefully creep around the dimly lit environments  just waiting for a zombie or mr  x to jump out at you  re3 remake on the other hand was a massive letdown  going into it  it would have been good to just have the atmosphere  puzzle solving and combat mechanics of re2 mixed in with a different story  characters  enemy types  environments and weapons  it seemed like it would be an easy  1  story   as far as i know the story is the same as in the original game and again is a pretty standard re offering  you play as jill valentine and after getting attacked by nemesis at her apartment  she flees into downtown raccoon city which is overrun by zombies  she eventually meets carlos  a u b c s soldier who becomes her ally and nicolai  another soldier who is eventually revealed to be the main human villain  whilst trying to avoid being killed by nemesis  the main plot involves jill and carlos trying to secure the t virus vaccine   about half the game takes place in downtown raccoon city before jill and carlos go to spencer memorial hospital  beneath which is an umbrella facility housing the vaccine  all the while nicolai is lurking about and at the end it is revealed that he wanted to record jil fighting nemesis so he could sell the recordings for profit  there are the usual boss battles against nemesis throughout the game and towards the end a race against the clock for our protagonists wherein they must escape raccoon city before it is nuked  in the end  jill defeats nemesis with a railgun  nicolai is wounded and left to die while jill and carlos escape in a helicopter  with jill vowing to do whatever it takes to take down umbrella    it s all very standard action movie stuff  as for the characters  there s not much to like  jill is a standard female action hero who has plenty of one liners but not much personality  no character arc  just a badass with an attitude  the main villain nicolai is super generic  nothing more than a mercenary who is willing to let millions die so that he can get paid  nothing we haven t seen in a thousand times before  the only character who i even remotely liked was carlos  his earnest and down to earth personality made you root for him  and he had some good banter with jill     i had to roll my eyes at the sequence wherein brad vickers  people who have played the original game will know who that is  shows up and attempts to get to safety with jill  not two minutes after meeting him he gets grabbed and eaten by a pack of zombies and the game shows it as being this highly dramatic moment of loss as jill loses a close friend  the problem being that we the audience have no sense of who this guy is as a character  he is just a blank slate that shows up one minute and is gone the next  i never played the original re3 and maybe he actually had some screentime and characterisation in that game but in the remake  he gets nothing  when you introduce a character into a story and you want their death to be impactful on the audience  you must put in the work of giving them some characteristics like motivations  goals and flaws  anyway  rip brad  i guess    2  combat   this game is obviously going for more of an action movie vibe as opposed to survival horror which is fine but the execution i feel is very lacking  the game makes it all too easy to progress through any given level  on regular difficulty  zombies die with approximately 3 to 4 shots to the head  which is super easy given that the game gives you a plethora of ammo even early in the game  there are also numerous explosive barrels scattered about in some of the more open areas which  when timed right  will kill 4 to 5 zombies at once  eliminating any kind of danger or tension in these zombie encounters     this is far from the experience of re2 remake  which has zero explosive barrels in it  as far as i can remember   in that game  it was always a case of here s a zombie  ok  do i kill it or try to go around it  do i have enough bullets to take it down  if i m going into a new  unknown area should i save my ammo and health items for then  the player soon learns the value of their limited resources in re2 and learns when and where to engage or avoid enemies  it pushes you to be careful about spending ammunition  so you might not want to kill all zombies that you encounter but at the same time if you leave enough alive  there s a good chance one will get you eventually  the player must factor all this in and then decide how they want to proceed     back to re3  it seems to me that the levels are so small  again  very rushed in development  you re finding ammo for your starter weapon on every street corner  i played this game on hardcore difficulty  and i was never worried at all about running out of ammo  for any of the weapons  the same goes for gunpowder and herbs as well  both being very plentiful   the normal zombie types are very easy to kill  again  you don t have to worry about ammo because you always have heaps and the wide open  well lit environments make it easy to spot a zombie at a safe distance and then take them down  no problem  no real tension  just a shooting gallery  especially once you play as carlos who has the fully automatic m4 rifle  with which you can just mow down everything in your path     3  nemesis   when compared to mr  x from re2r  nemesis is a good example of why sometimes less is more  like the original game  his sole mission is to kill jill valentine  throughout the early sections of the game  he crashes through walls  jumps from buildings and at one point can walk through fire unscathed  this game reiterates the old cliche of the protagonist getting knocked down and then the villain slowly walking towards them and then something or someone distracts the villain so that the protagonist survives  this happens with nemesis and jill a lot  in a few subpar cutscenes where the game really tries to make nemesis look cool and menacing but for me at least it just comes off as hollow   you don t dread nemesis the way you dread mr  x  what with his blank  unemotional demeanour and the way he just keeps coming after you like the t 800  nothing beats creeping around rpd while hearing mr  x s footsteps echoing all around you     the boss fights are pretty mediocre when compared to the re series  best  the first one is a basic run and gun wherein you fight nemesis on a rooftop until you get knocked off said roof onto a fire escape below  the only thing that sets this fight apart from the usual shooting and avoiding getting hit is that you can shoot a fuel barrel on nemesis s back to deal him a good chunk of damage  the next two are strangely similar wherein you fight nemesis in his second form in a cylindrical shaped environment  a plaza with a fountain and then a massive lab containment chamber  and these two fights play out pretty much the same  there s the usual shooting and chasing and then nemesis takes to the walls of the area and runs around in circles before jumping down on top you to deal some damage  the only real difference is that in the second fight you can shoot some poles that electrocute nemesis and slow him down  the final fight is probably the best one wherein the player must dodge the fully mutated and tentacled mass of nemesis and attempt to restore power to a railgun which the player can then use to kill him once and for all  for me it was still pretty underwhelming but so is the entire game    all up  2 10  play re2 remake instead

resident evil 3 remake  2020  – score  7 5 10  despite hearing a lot of criticism from friends  i finally decided to try this remake  as the original re3 was my first experience with the series and holds a special place in my memory   i bought the resident evil games on steam to complete the collection and replay some titles before re9  and grabbed this one for r 22 on sale   the game s biggest flaw is its short length — i finished it in 4 hours and 35 minutes on my first playthrough  many parts from the original were cut  but if you treat it as a  new  resident evil  it s a fun game   the graphics are excellent  it runs smoothly  voice acting is great  and both jill and carlos are well reimagined  secondary characters like tyrell and nicholai also got solid updates  i especially liked the hospital segment   on the downside  the nemesis encounters are mostly scripted and lack the tension of his unpredictable appearances in the original  the city exploration is also more linear and less engaging   overall  it s a good action game with survival horror elements  it s fun  but as a remake  it feels less memorable than the original — and that shouldn t be the case    resident evil 3  remake  2020  nota  7 5 10  depois de meus amigos falarem tão mal do remake  eu decidi postergar bem a gameplay dele  pois o 3 original foi meu primeiro contato com capcom s resident evil 3 remake is a masterclass in action horror that delivers nonstop tension  stunning visuals  and unforgettable encounters – but what truly elevates it is its incredible replayability and the often overlooked multiplayer experience  from the moment jill valentine steps into the ravaged streets of raccoon city  the game grips you with explosive pacing and relentless terror  nemesis is a formidable evolution of mr  x  dynamically pushing players to stay alert and adapt constantly  but while the campaign may be shorter than its predecessor  its tight structure and smooth combat make it perfect for multiple replays  unlockable items  speedrun incentives  higher difficulty tiers  and in game shop rewards keep the experience feeling fresh and rewarding  what many skip past is the multiplayer companion mode  resident evil resistance  which deserves far more credit  it introduces an asymmetric 4v1 setup where one player becomes a sadistic mastermind while others band together as survivors trying to escape  it s chaotic  unpredictable  and surprisingly strategic – a real gem for fans wanting to extend their re3 experience beyond the main story  whether you re a newcomer or a diehard fan  resident evil 3 remake offers a high octane  replayable survival horror adventure backed by one of the most creative multiplayer twists the franchise has explored  with slick mechanics  jaw dropping graphics  and a perfect balance between fear and firepower  this is easily a 10 10 must play 
resident evil 3  nemesis sometimes feels like the black sheep of the original playstation trilogy  it s been very well received in past and contemporary times and was a massive sales success  still  despite being a great game  i can t help but feel like many of its elements feel strange compared to its immediate predecessors  the path choice system  the copious amounts of rng  and its development history as a spin off title add to this feeling  naturally  the idea of remaking re3 n  while not entirely necessary  is enticing  how do you adapt its quirks into a modern context while improving upon its admittedly minor flaws  apparently  according to capcom  the answer was  you don t  the resident evil 3 remake isn t even close to being as bad as some fans make it out to be  but it is a very disappointing game nonetheless  i ve never played a game that simultaneously felt so polished and yet so incomplete  while it does manage to improve upon the original game in some ways  by and large  it serves as a massive step back from both the original re3 and its immediate predecessor  the resident evil 2 remake   from the onset  it s clear that re3make is different in tone from re2make  with a breakneck pace that seldom offers the player any respite  there s little room for re2make s lonely hall wandering or silent tension when the game grabs you by the neck from the first moment of real gameplay and drags you along for seven odd hours  this lends itself to an entirely different tone and while i don t necessarily dislike it  i do think the game could have benefitted from more moments of silent tension  which feel almost absent  on a game design level  re3make is for the most part fairly faithful to re3 n  preserving the more action oriented style of gameplay while remaining directly tied to core survival horror design philosophies  this isn t a downright action horror game  though it does border on it at points  changes from re2make such as the knife being invincible  the dodge mechanic  and the lack of ink ribbons on first time playthroughs make re3make a fairly unchallenging game even on hardcore difficulty compared to the brutality of re2make s hardcore mode  despite this  it s a very polished and fun gameplay experience that while somewhat breezy is still one i enjoyed playing through  the dodge mechanic is one of the few things this game does much better than re3 n  being infamously finicky in that game and much smoother to execute in this one  it s satisfying to accomplish and it encourages the player to stay away from enemies rather than engaging them directly  the hunter betas  in particular  are designed heavily around this system  and either dodging their attacks as jill or countering them with a punch from carlos is satisfying even with the threat they represent  i just wish the game was more punishing towards the player   you re given tons of dangerous weapons very early on that can almost trivialize many encounters  the hunter gammas are cool  but when they die in two hits to flame rounds the game keeps handing to me  their threat level is greatly reduced  the lack of ink ribbons or any kind of save limiting feature means that there s no consequence other than time to just saving as much as you want  and no strategy  it s still a very polished and enjoyable time  but it lacks the gameplay sophistication that re2make has   the elephant in the room here is how re3make handles accuracy to the source material  you ve probably heard a thousand times that this is a severely cut down remake and you d be entirely correct  the biggest disappointment here is how nemesis t type  the tyrant that at least narratively stalks you throughout the whole game  is handled  nemesis in re3 n is infamously a case of carefully crafted smoke and mirrors  most encounters with him are mandatory  and outside of a few areas that he  patrols  for the player being based around rng  he is less dynamic and random than most people remember  however  the reason why nemesis struck fear into the hearts of players more than re2 s mr  x was that the mixture of genuine randomness and scripted encounters was done in a manner that felt convincing  it genuinely felt like nemesis was hunting jill down and would not stop at any cost like you were getting to play your own terminator film  this is not the case at all in re3make  where nemesis feels more scripted than ever  while i remember having a few random encounters with nemesis while playing the raccoon city demo that was released a month before the game s release  in the finished game i never encountered him outside of what felt like explicitly scripted moments  perhaps i was just genuinely unlucky  but there appear to be very few spots where encountering nemesis is a chance and not a guarantee  the boss battles with him  especially the rooftop flamethrower fight  are great  making great use of the game s mechanics alongside making him  if for a moment  feel intimidating and threatening  almost every encounter with him that isn t a boss fight  however  feels like a playable cinematic cutscene from a naughty dog game or something  that s a fine thing to have occasionally  but when it consists of the majority of his encounters  this supposed ultimate bioweapon feels far weaker than re2make s terrifying mr  x  the exact opposite of what happened with the psx games  the game also cuts many memorable and iconic locations from the original  such as the cemetery  clock tower  and dead factory  with the grave digger cut too  unfortunately  without replacing them with anything similarly memorable  nest 2 is cool and all  but come on  it s no dead factory  remakes will always inevitably cut things  after all  it s a brand new game and not just the original prettied up  but how re3make flagrantly throws out core aspects of the game it s based on it almost feels disrespectful even if that s not intentional  most of its setpieces are perfectly fine if not outright good  but none of them reach the same iconic heights as the original   i had to cut out a lot of this review due to valve s text guidelines  let me assure you this game is decent but disappointing   resident evil 3 remake is a solid game in many departments  it plays great  with fun gameplay mechanics and boss fights that offer a tense  exhilarating experience  the game features many writing upgrades over the original  great graphics with a lot of technical quality  and just generally is a polished  bug free  and well made game  unfortunately  the game doesn t even come close to living up to the lofty expectations of the classic 1999 survival horror game and it suffers from many deep flaws  nemesis has been kneecapped  feeling less dynamic than the original  90s psx game  many memorable and iconic locations are cut from the game without any sort of equivalent replacement  a serious lack of difficulty even on hardcore mode  a version of jill that is less interesting and developed  impressive graphical features of the prior game have been heavily downgraded  and the score is not as oppressive as the original or as atmospheric as it s immediate predecessor  it s still an alright game despite this  i had my fun with it and i can see myself revisiting it at some point  but i struggle to recommend this past re fans like myself 
resident evil 3   fast  furious  and… a little trimmed  resident evil 3  2020  is a remake of the classic game resident evil 3  nemesis  and like the resident evil 2 remake  it offers a refreshed look at a familiar story  but with some compromises  the game delivers a more action oriented gameplay experience than its predecessor  immersing you in the chaotic streets of raccoon city  consumed by the zombie apocalypse   what s good   •  thrilling action  re3  2020  focuses on dynamic action  battles against zombies and mutants have become more intense and spectacular  dodging  counter attacking  and using the environment play an important role in survival  •  superb graphics and sound  the game looks stunning  thanks to the re engine  the environment of raccoon city is recreated with incredible detail  and the zombies and monsters look disgustingly realistic  the sound design creates a tense and terrifying atmosphere  •  great performance by jill valentine  jill is portrayed as a strong and determined heroine who is interesting to watch  her interaction with other characters  especially carlos oliveira  looks believable and convincing  •  nemesis – a nightmare come to life  nemesis remains the scariest pursuer in the series  he constantly hunts you  adapts to your actions  and uses various weapons to destroy you  every encounter with him is a test of your skills and nerves   what s not so good   •  short length  this is perhaps the biggest drawback of the game  a playthrough only takes 5 7 hours  which is significantly less than the original game and the resident evil 2 remake  •  cut content  the remake is missing some locations and bosses from the original game  this is disappointing  especially for those familiar with the original  •  fewer puzzles  re3  2020  emphasizes action  resulting in fewer puzzles that are simpler than in resident evil 2  •  more linear level design  unlike the more open structure of re2  re3  2020  offers a more linear and predictable level design  •  resistance multiplayer adds no value  the resistance mode  although trying to bring something new  feels rushed and doesn t offer enough depth to keep players engaged   in conclusion   resident evil 3  2020  is a thrilling and visually impressive remake that offers excellent action and a creepy atmosphere  however  its short length and abundance of cut content make it less significant than the resident evil 2 remake  if you are looking for a dynamic and adrenaline pumping horror experience  then you will enjoy re3  2020   but if you were expecting a deeper and more prolonged game  you may be disappointed   recommendations   •  worth buying if you are a resident evil fan and love action  •  wait for a sale if you are concerned about the short length  •  don t expect a repeat of the resident evil 2 remake triumph   score   7 5 10  resident evil 3  2020  is a decent  but not perfect  remake that offers exciting action and a terrifying atmosphere  but suffers from short length and cut content 
remember that time you were just trying to leave town  and an 8 foot tall bio engineered abomination punched you through a wall  yeah  jill valentine does too   this remake takes the 1999 classic  trims the fat  adds some gym gains to nemesis  and throws you into a zombie infested raccoon city with barely enough time to scream  it s fast  it s loud  and nemesis treats doors like mere suggestions   🧟‍♂️ what i loved  ✅ jill valentine  certified badass  sarcasm dealer  apocalypse fashion icon ✅ combat feels chef s kiss—headshots are crunchy  dodging is slick ✅ graphics so good i apologized to my monitor ✅ nemesis doesn t chase you everywhere  just most places  like an overly attached ex with a rocket launcher  ✅ tons of replayability thanks to in game shop and challenges  😬 what could be better  ❌ short campaign—you can finish it before your pizza delivery arrives ❌ some fan favorite areas got thanos snapped out of existence ❌ more action  less horror—but hey  explosions are scary too  this isn t the slow burn creep fest of re2 remake  re3 is more like if john wick was fighting zombies and had to deal with a guy made of leftover tentacles and rage issues   so if you re into high stakes zombie escapes  jill s savage one liners  and getting tackled by nemesis like it s the nfl playoffs  give this one a go   💬 final thoughts  come for the zombies  stay for the sass  and try not to scream when nemesis kool aid man s through a wall yelling  s t a r s  
my take let s get this out of the way  i loved how jill valentine carried this game  she s an absolute badass  and her redesigned outfit  no tube top  finally   made her feel like a believable survivor   the game felt like a hollywood action flick—explosions  chases  and set pieces that kept me glued even on my potato pc  yes  13fps struggles are real   the short runtime  honestly  it worked for me  i binged it in two sittings  obsessed with finishing despite technical hiccups  but i get why die hard fans might feel shortchanged     pros to play jill valentine   carlos redesigns jill s modern look is practical and badass  ditching the original s questionable fashion choices  carlos also gets a glow up  blending charisma with combat grit  their dynamic adds heart to the chaos    nemesis  terrifying but underutilized    the first encounter with nemesis is chef s kiss—he sprints  leaps  and wields a flamethrower like a nightmare come to life  while his later appearances feel scripted  those initial moments are pure adrenaline      cinematic action   set pieces    if you re here for spectacle  this delivers  the subway escape  rooftop battles  and a raiders of the lost ark style boulder chase had me grinning  it s more michael bay than classic survival horror  but i m not mad      dodge mechanics   combat flow    the dodge system  especially the slow mo “perfect dodge”  feels slick  pair that with the infinite knife for zombie kneecapping  and combat stays tense yet empowering      replayability   speedrunning    short  yes  but that makes it perfect for replaying  i knocked out a 4 hour run easily  and the ranking system  based on time saves  hooks you into optimizing routes     cons to consider 1  too short  too linear    clocking in at  6 hours  the campaign lacks the depth of re2 remake  locations like the clock tower and park from the original are missing  replaced with linear corridors      2  nemesis  wasted potential    after the first act  he s reduced to scripted boss fights  fans expected mr  x level persistence  instead  he s more of a jump scare cameo      3  survival horror  more like action horror    limited puzzles  no window boarding mechanics  and fewer backtracking opportunities  the focus on action might alienate fans craving slow burn tension      4  performance issues    my sub geforce gt rig wheezed through this  but even optimized setups note inconsistent physics  zombies feel less “weighty” than re2  and jarring dodge animations      5  resistance multiplayer  forgettable    the tacked on 4v1 mode is unbalanced and lacks staying power  stick to the campaign    final verdict i recommend it  if you treat it as a cinematic spin off rather than a faithful remake  re3 shines  jill s charisma  nemesis  early scares  and the rollercoaster pacing make it a blast—especially on sale  just don t expect re2 remake s depth    for  action lovers  jill stans  speedrunners    avoid if  you crave classic survival horror or hate short games  play it like i did  embrace the chaos  ignore the purists  and enjoy jill s shotgun wielding glory  and maybe upgrade your gpu first

resident evil 3 is one of the greatest survival horror games of all time  it balances action and horror brilliantly  features a badass returning protagonist from another one of the best survival horror games  and introduces the grandfather of the stalker enemy   of course  the review above was for the original resident evil 3  the remake is a very different game   resident evil 3 remake falls short of its predecessor in the following categories  costumes  the original resident evil 3 had eight alternate costumes  while the remake has    2  in addition to the lower amount of costumes the costumes themselves aren t terrible  but they definitely aren t as exciting as the originals dino crisis outfit  as the remake features the worst adaptation of the of re3 outfit  though it does feature  in my opinion  one of the better s t a r s outfits being the first time said outfits been featured in a modern title  locations  the game omits several locations from the original such as city hall  the press office  the raccoon city park  the graveyard  the dead factory  and most jarringly  the clocktower  these commissions leave the remake world version of raccoon city empty and unsatisfactory  enemies  just like the resident evil 2 remake  the resident evil 3 remake forgets the giant spiders  while also getting rid of the gravedigger boss fight  leaving nemesis as the only boss in the game  nemesis  the biggest flaw in my opinion is that nemesis is no longer a stalker enemy  he is scripted and only had one area of the game where he was sort of a stalker  but i don t find this to be too harrowing considering the re  remake turned mr  x  a scripted enemy  into a stalker enemy  so between the games mr  x and nemesis changed dynamics  which certainly isn t the worst that can happen   i just listed a bunch of things i think the resident evil 3 remake doesn t do the best  so what about the things it does excel at  characters  various characters are portrayed so much better in the remake  jill hasn t worsened or improved in my opinion  she is still a badass who suffers only slightly from not having a definitive spencer mansion background in the remake universe yet  carlos went from being an unremarkable sideshow to a keanu reeves style action star with relatable dialogue as he gets as frustrated with puzzles as the player does  in addition to having pretty neat hair  nikoli is a delightful villain who gets plenty of scenes of being a general nuisance and outright unhinged individual with little regard for others  and while his end is a little lackluster  he was entertaining at the very least  mikhail doesn t have much screen time  but he makes the most of it by his determination to rescue those around him  and in his final moments he stands up valiantly to nemesis  tyrell is another character that has a smaller presence  but does his best in his moments to do right and also sacrifices himself to give jill time to escape her tyrant stalker  gameplay  the game has very similar gameplay to the re2 remake  but with the addition of dodging  which can be finicky  but if pulled off right can be very beneficial to gameplay  the perfect dodge also slows down time and auto aims at enemies weak points for an extra edge in combat  extra items  extra items in re games are always delightful  but the ones introduced in re3 remake are some of my favorites  the hot dogger and raiden look like they re ripped straight out of a sci fi game and the combat manual  and medallions make the game easier by increasing perfect dodge windows and applying bonuses to combat  defense  and healing  enemies  i know i had a section for enemies in the negatives  but while the game did remove some  it also added some  such as replacing the brain suckers with drain deimos  that are part of the scariest section in the game  the ne α parasite zombie  which has a large promiscuously designed parasite attached to its head that can attack from a distance and up close  and the hunter gamma  which is a fusion of the gravedigger and hunter beta    ultimately the game has its flaws  yes  but it isn t a terrible game  on the contrary  i find it to be as enjoyable as the re2 remake  if anyone finds themselves debating on wether or not to get the game  my advice is if you like resident evil  if you like horror  if you like zombies  then you ll like reident evil 3 remake

resident evil 3   by triple f  the 2020 remake of  resident evil 3  by capcom offers a modern  action packed reimagining of the 1999 classic  revisiting jill valentine s harrowing escape from a crumbling raccoon city  released on the heels of the successful  resident evil 2  remake   resident evil 3  aims to capture the terror and intensity of its original while updating graphics  mechanics  and storytelling to align with modern standards  the result is a thrilling experience that emphasizes faster gameplay and cinematic moments but sacrifices some of the depth and exploration that made its predecessor so engaging   in terms of visuals   resident evil 3  shines  using capcom s re engine to deliver impressive  realistic graphics that bring raccoon city to life in disturbing detail  from abandoned streets to dimly lit corridors  each location captures a sense of decay and danger  immersing players in the nightmare  jill valentine s character model is updated with a practical  grounded look that reflects her status as a battle hardened survivor  and her interactions with carlos oliveira and the members of umbrella s biohazard countermeasure service add personality and weight to the story  the level of detail in both character models and environmental design amplifies the horror  making every area feel immersive and foreboding   the gameplay in  resident evil 3  leans into an action oriented approach  distinguishing itself from the slower  more methodical pace of  resident evil 2   jill is agile  equipped with a dodge mechanic that adds a satisfying layer to combat by allowing skilled players to avoid enemy attacks and counter with precision  the combat itself is polished  with a variety of weapons available to handle hordes of zombies  as well as more lethal creatures like hunters and the ever present nemesis  this faster paced gameplay  while exciting  edges the game closer to action horror  reducing some of the tension associated with traditional survival horror  where every encounter is meant to feel weighty and dangerous   nemesis  one of the franchise s most iconic antagonists  serves as the heart of  resident evil 3   relentlessly pursuing jill through raccoon city  while nemesis appears in scripted sequences rather than dynamic encounters  his presence is still formidable  forcing players into intense  high stakes confrontations  his adaptability  like using tentacles to ensnare jill or even wielding a rocket launcher  creates thrilling encounters that keep players on their toes  however  some fans of the original may miss the unpredictability of his appearances  as the scripted nature of these moments makes him feel more like a series of set pieces than a constant  looming threat    resident evil 3  s focus on linear progression over exploration marks a departure from the layered design of  resident evil 2   while there are some opportunities for backtracking and finding optional items  the game primarily pushes players through a sequence of high stakes moments  emphasizing pace over environmental complexity  this streamlined approach makes the game feel more action driven  with less emphasis on puzzle solving and resource management  although this structure keeps the momentum high  some players may find that it limits the game s sense of discovery and depth  as exploration and puzzle elements have traditionally been a core part of the resident evil experience   despite its strengths   resident evil 3  has a notably short campaign  clocking in around five to six hours for most players  even on a first playthrough  while the game s pacing is tight  the shorter length may leave some players wanting more  especially when compared to the more robust content in  resident evil 2   to address replay ability  capcom included “resident evil resistance ” a 4v1 multiplayer mode where one player acts as the mastermind  setting traps and controlling zombies to thwart four survivors  while resistance offers an interesting twist on multiplayer horror  it doesn t quite compensate for the brevity of the main campaign  particularly for fans seeking a deeper single player experience   overall   resident evil 3   2020  is an intense  visually stunning remake that successfully modernizes the original s action horror thrills  while it lacks the slower paced  exploration heavy elements of previous titles  it delivers an adrenaline fueled journey through raccoon city  packed with memorable encounters and cinematic moments  for fans who appreciate the franchise s evolution toward more action oriented gameplay   resident evil 3  is a worthwhile experience  however  those who miss the intricate puzzles and open ended exploration that define the series  roots may find this installment falls short of the classic survival horror formula 
welcome to racoon city  you re about to die     hurry up jill  kill the nemesis  move your buttocks out of the city    resident evil 3 remake  a thrilling dash through raccoon city s doom  and jill s denim excellence   capcom s resident evil 3 remake takes us back to raccoon city for another pulse pounding fight against the undead  with a protagonist whose courage—and curves—can t be stopped  jill valentine isn t just running from nemesis  she s running straight into action  armed with a glock  an attitude  and a rear view so iconic it deserves its own credit in the ending sequence   plot  zombies  explosions  and a clock ticking toward inevitable obliteration the story kicks off with jill attempting to escape raccoon city s zombie apocalypse while being hunted by nemesis  a bioweapon so persistent it makes your clingiest ex look like a saint  as she dodges zombies  lickers  and an absurd number of explosive barrels  jill s fight for survival feels like a race against the inevitable—because let s face it  raccoon city s ultimate fate is less a mystery and more a foregone conclusion   yes  the nuke is coming  the only question is whether you can outrun it—and let s be real  if anyone can sprint through an apocalypse while looking that good  it s jill valentine   gameplay  tight action  tighter jeans the remake leans hard into action  with combat that feels punchy and fluid  dodging nemesis s tentacle attacks  thrilling  watching jill roll out of the way with precision that somehow highlights her impeccable jeans  unfairly captivating   the addition of a perfect dodge mechanic gives you a split second opportunity to avoid danger—while also giving players plenty of moments to appreciate jill s  uh  tactical flexibility  let s just say capcom s re engine is working overtime  both in horror and posterior rendering   nemesis  the ultimate simp nemesis is an unrelenting force  smashing through walls and chasing jill like he s auditioning for a horror themed dating show  but despite his monstrous presence  there s something almost comical about how laser focused he is on one woman  maybe he just appreciates jill s  let s say  well rounded skill set   of course  his boss battles are nerve wracking  but they pale in comparison to the sheer tension of hearing him mutter “stars” from somewhere just out of sight   pro tip  if nemesis ever looks like he s about to swing that rocket launcher  just assume there s another explosion inbound  seriously  who gave this guy so many explosives    deep joke  a nuclear pun  why is jill always calm under pressure  because she knows no matter what happens  the nuke will clean up the mess  raccoon city  a doomed playground the setting is beautifully realized  though every alley and sewer reeks of inevitable destruction  you ll explore the city s ruins  from neon lit streets to umbrella s grimy labs  but the sense of impending doom never fades  it s like window shopping for chaos  knowing everything s going to be in flames by morning   character chemistry  jill and carlos  the odd couple carlos oliveira  jill s backup  and occasional flirt partner   adds some levity with his banter  his mop of perfect hair and cheesy one liners contrast beautifully with jill s no nonsense determination  but let s not kid ourselves  jill is the star here  carrying the game with her grit  wit  and gravity defying wardrobe choices   graphics  explosions and    attention to detail the re engine once again proves its mastery  rendering every explosion  shambling zombie  and perfectly placed pocket on jill s jeans with astonishing detail  even as the city burns and nemesis roars  you can t help but notice how well everything is    constructed   final thoughts  a blast in more ways than one resident evil 3 remake is a thrilling rollercoaster through chaos  destruction  and some very memorable    visuals  jill valentine cements her place as one of gaming s most badass  and fabulously virus spread  then to the laboratory which was completely destroyed to the strange sewers  giving a new feel from re 2 which is only in certain locations •	the two characters jill and carlos are also interesting and unique characters  we are given a view of how two characters who are equally professional and strong can face the enemy  unlike re 2 who are still rookies here because they are professionals they can do many things like dodge and hit and are more agile and have lots of action •	there are lots of new enemy variations  from ordinary zombies to nemesis who are super strong and the details  basically everything in the environment blends together perfectly  there s no loading screen  except the one you get when load into the game   •	satisfying ending  cons  •	very short game time  i can finish this in just 5 hours  i really wish this series was longer than re 2 but it s actually quicker and feels rushed on several occasions •	too many unnecessary cutscenes •	there are a few puzzles to be had  i also like the puzzles in re 2 but here they are very few and very easy to solve •	the horror aspect is very lacking  instead there is a lot of action  they should do this in a balanced manner  because the game actually falls not like a horror game but an action game •	the overall map of raccoon city is now limited to just 2 streets and like 3 hallways  this is supposed to be the racoon city game  but the city segment is so short that is really sad  all the locations are there but they are just cameos and references •	boring fight boss scene  conclusion   apart from the title  a stunning revival   resident evil 3 remake  resident evil 3 remake is a masterful reimagining of the classic 1999 survival horror title  and it has undoubtedly set a new standard for remakes in the gaming industry  from the moment you launch the game  you are greeted with an atmospheric blend of tension  action  and nostalgia that makes this experience feel both fresh and familiar   visuals and atmosphere  first and foremost  the graphics are nothing short of breathtaking  the environments are richly detailed  from the ruined streets of raccoon city to the eerie corridors of the nemesis  lair  the lighting effects create an immersive ambiance that heightens the horror elements  making you feel every shadow creep and every unsettling noise in your bones  the character models are incredibly lifelike  and both jill valentine and carlos oliveira are portrayed with depth and nuance  perfectly capturing their roles in the story   gameplay mechanics  the gameplay in resident evil 3 remake strikes a superb balance between action and classic survival horror elements  the controls are intuitive  making it easy to maneuver through tense situations while still offering a satisfying challenge  the introduction of the dodge mechanic elevates the gameplay  allowing for nail biting encounters with enemies  facing off against the iconic nemesis is a highlight  as he tracks jill relentlessly and forces players to adapt their strategies on the fly  these intense chase sequences are adrenaline pumping and are beautifully crafted to keep you on the edge of your seat   story and character development  the narrative in resident evil 3 remake is compelling  with a well paced storyline that expertly weaves together action and lore  jill s character development is particularly noteworthy  you get to witness her transformation from a survivor to a determined fighter  the voice acting is superb  with notable performances that add depth to the storytelling  the dialogue is sharp  and the interactions between characters feel genuine  adding to the emotional stakes of the game   sound design and music  the sound design plays a crucial role in establishing the atmosphere of fear and dread throughout your playthrough  from the distant growls of zombies to the heavy footfalls of nemesis  every sound is meticulously crafted to enhance the experience  coupled with a haunting soundtrack that dynamically shifts with the gameplay  resident evil 3 remake delivers a fully immersive audio experience that keeps you engulfed in tension   quality of life improvements  the remake incorporates several quality of life improvements that enhance gameplay  the inventory system is streamlined  making it easier to manage your items and ammunition  the addition of safe rooms and map hints helps to alleviate some of the frustration that can come from navigating in survival horror games  allowing players to focus on the horror elements without feeling overwhelmed   final thoughts  resident evil 3 remake is not just a tribute to its predecessor  it is a bold reinvention that breathes new life into the franchise  it expertly balances nostalgia with fresh gameplay mechanics and delivers an utterly thrilling experience from start to finish  whether you are a longtime fan or new to the series  this game deserves a spot in your collection  it s a rollercoaster ride of scares and excitement that will leave you eager for more  capcom has truly earned its place in the spotlight with this outstanding remake  and it stands as a must play title for any video game enthusiast

a remake of the original re3 game  this game offers gorgeous graphics and immersive audio themes to pull you into the world of nemesis supremacy  a 3rd person horror survival shooter game  the player is able to use a spread of weapons and tools to escape a zombie infested city before succumbing to the evil around them    combat is centered around careful marksmanship and optimizing specific weapons against certain enemies  of course  the standard run and gun or spray and pray styles still work  but will quickly deplete your resources  leaving you awkwardly evading enemies as you try to run around them   the dodge mechanic can be a real hit or miss for most players  and should best be used as a panic escape option when enemies are in your face  don t rely on it to get you out of trouble  however  balanced this way i m assuming   as positioning is your best ally    graphics wise  this game is beautiful  even when turned down in quality  the skin water sheen appearance in particular is quite attractive  leading to a large number of  popular  alterations on the web  nevertheless  the character details  actions  maybe not the mouth movements   and environment are all pleasing to say the least  enemies  too  look quite nice  especially during combat and close up animations  and the cutscenes  phew  maybe a little too much budget  but i ain t complaining  the only problem i noted occasionally were long distance graphics  as they would tear in and out  however  those were often the shadows of building signs or smoke  so not terribly important during your playthroughs    audio wise  a notch above standard  but not spectacularly out there  different bgm s serve to help immerse the player in a particular environment or game theme  and while the chosen tracks fit the gameplay  they are not  the  soundtrack  if you understand what i m trying to say  still  quite immersive during the run throughs  and special shout out to the electrical plant and hospital areas    quality of life wise  the game is quite generous  offering players plenty of loot and supplies if one was to actually search  the earnable shop rewards are quite fun as well  and almost a requirement on the higher difficulties  makes the game a bit  easy  so to speak  but nothing says you have to use those infinite rockets  loading sections are quite fast  with many loads often taking place during extended map sections  elevators  long corridors  etc   and menu interactions are basic but straightforward   infrastructure wise  the game is quite stable  as i did not experience a single crash or even a bug during my series of playthroughs  and was very consistent with its decently fast loading sections  maybe a few laggy areas  but very rare  and was often due to auto saving or a sudden reveal of a large map enemy  as with most games nowadays  high temperatures are most often the biggest issue  and i was hovering in the 50 degrees celsius region while on undervolting  i use throttlestop   definitely keep an eye on your pc temps  i use core temp  as i am most certain your fans will be running at nearly full speed    a somewhat short but very stable resident evil  a  solid  game  nothing too world breaking  but this game does its job  might not win game of the year award  but still fun to play through  in my opinion  immersion is probably this game s strongest suit  but if the devs are reading these  a great job nonetheless  and i look forward to continuing to support your future works

as a connoisseur of fine digital entertainment  i have taken it upon myself to delve into the latest iteration of the resident evil franchise  resident evil 3  in my esteemed opinion  this game boasts several strengths  punctuated by innovative features that breathe new life into the series  admittedly  it is somewhat disheartening to observe that certain content from the original has been excised  however  this does not detract from the overall quality or enjoyment of the game   resident evil 3 leans more heavily towards action  yet it masterfully retains the tension that is the hallmark of the series  some may argue that the game is lamentably brief  possibly the shortest among the modern resident evil titles  nonetheless  i find that this brevity can be viewed as a strength  the adage  short and sweet  aptly applies here  in a landscape where many games are lengthy and exhausting  it is refreshing to encounter a title that can be completed in a single  exhilarating session   given its relatively concise duration  it might be prudent to acquire this game during a discount period  this way  one can fully appreciate its merits without reservations  ultimately  resident evil 3 is a worthy addition to the franchise  offering a compact yet thrilling experience that is both invigorating and satisfying
