role from the 1999 classic  jill valentine  a former s t a r s  member  fights for survival amidst the chaos  unlike the more methodical exploration of the spencer mansion in the first remake  re3 is a frantic race against time    h2 story  a desperate escape with unforgettable characters  h2  the narrative retains the core beats of the original while injecting fresh scares and emotional depth  jill is a fantastic protagonist – resourceful  determined  and voiced with impressive conviction by nicole tompkins  we see her vulnerability alongside her strength  making her a character you truly root for   carlos oliveira  a u b c s  mercenary jill encounters  is another highlight  their contrasting personalities create a dynamic duo  with carlos offering both humor and a foil to jill s seriousness  the remake wisely expands on carlos s role  fleshing out his motivations and making him a more compelling character   the story throws some curveballs at veterans of the original  keeping you on your toes  it doesn t shy away from the series  trademark horror  with grotesque enemies and unsettling environments  there s a genuine sense of desperation as jill navigates a decaying city crawling with the undead  stalked by the relentless nemesis    h2 gameplay  a thrilling dance with danger  h2  re3 embraces action oriented survival horror  dodging and maneuvering become crucial as you face hordes of the infected  ammo scarcity adds tension  forcing you to prioritize targets and make every bullet count  the remake streamlines some puzzles compared to the original  but they remain well designed and integrated with the environment   the constant threat of nemesis elevates the gameplay  this hulking bioweapon is relentless  forcing you to adapt your strategies on the fly   whether it s bursting through walls or unleashing devastating tentacles  nemesis injects a constant dose of adrenaline into the experience    h2 a feast for the eyes and ears  h2  the re engine continues to impress  raccoon city is a decaying metropolis brought to life in horrifying detail  the lighting effects are particularly impressive  creating a grimy  oppressive atmosphere that perfectly complements the game s horror elements  the sound design is equally top notch  with the moans of the infected and the pounding soundtrack amplifying the tension    h2 overall  a must play for horror fans and re veterans  h2  resident evil 3 is a fantastic reimagining of a classic  it delivers an intense  action packed experience with a compelling narrative and unforgettable characters  the visuals and sound design are top notch  creating a truly immersive atmosphere     b recommendation   if you re looking for a thrilling escape from raccoon city  filled with heart pounding action and genuine scares  then grab your jill valentine cosplay and dive into resident evil 3  you won t regret it  unless  of course  you encounter nemesis    b

 in re3 remake  there are no alternative characters to play as  in re1 remake and re2 remake  i started a second playthrough right away  the other character provided their own story to experience in a uninspired copies of earlier settings  the downtown starting area is fun to explore  but you cannot return to finish exploring it  that i found   i had 2 of the 3 jewels in my pocket to deposit in a single incident that exemplifies the numerous scripted encounters that are no more than cutscenes that provide the illusion of playing a game via instruction of moving the joystick or hitting a button   those mechanics are good in other games but not something that works here    the sewers and laboratory environments are retreads of areas in the re2 remake  which wouldn t be too much of an issue if dodge buttons  after the first few  i audibly groaned when he d pop up for another spout of failed revenge  there is no fun factor or innovation that builds upon the last encounter  unlike re2 remake which lets you search environments after defeating the boss to pick up items you may not have collected  re3 remake forces you into yet another cutscene right away ultimately losing valuable resources physical prowess  if video game protagonists were like our real life fleshy bodies  there wouldn t be a game as they d outright die in the first scene  with that being said  re3 remake does go too far when considering the genre  a survival game needs more grounded action that reflects the consequences of bodily harm and mental trauma  a horror game loses menace when the protagonist survives every horrific encounter via a cutscene  nemesis becomes an incompetent saturday morning cartoon villain who fails at his one job over and over again         there are some annoying story cliches too  jill a heart pounding zombie smackdown    hey there  fellow gamers  i just had to drop a line about my recent experience with resident evil 3 remake  and boy  oh boy  was it a wild ride  if you re a fan of survival horror and adrenaline pumping action  this game is an absolute must play   first things first  let s talk graphics  they re off the charts  the level of detail in the environments  from the desolate streets of raccoon city to the eerie interiors of the abandoned buildings  is jaw dropping  i swear  i could almost smell the decay in the air  the character models are insanely realistic too  with every sweat bead and muscle twitch making me feel like i was right there alongside jill valentine   speaking of jill  she s a total badass in this remake  she s not just a pretty face  folks  armed with her trusty weapons and unwavering determination  she takes on hordes of grotesque zombies and terrifying creatures with style  the gameplay mechanics are smooth as butter  allowing for precise shooting and heart pounding escapes  oh  and let s not forget the explosive boss battles that had me on the edge of my seat  frantically strategizing every move   the pacing of the game is spot on  it s a rollercoaster of tension and release  constantly keeping you engaged  you ll explore creepy locations  solve puzzles  some of them had me scratching my head for a while   and encounter unexpected surprises around every corner  the sense of dread and urgency never lets up  making for an intense and immersive experience from start to finish   now  let s talk about the audio design  the atmospheric soundtrack perfectly complements the nightmarish setting  setting the mood for pure terror  every creak  growl  and scream had me jumping out of my skin  and the voice acting  top notch  the characters felt alive  or undead  in some cases  with their realistic emotions and reactions  kudos to the voice actors for bringing them to life   while the game s campaign might be relatively short  the replayability factor is through the roof  there are multiple difficulty levels to challenge your skills  and the unlockable bonuses and achievements will keep completionists busy for hours on end  plus  there s an addictive multiplayer mode called resistance that adds a whole new dimension of fun and chaos   all in all  resident evil 3 remake is a blast from start to finish  it s a nerve wracking  pulse pounding experience that will make you appreciate the survival horror genre all over again  with its stunning visuals  tight gameplay mechanics  and captivating storyline  this game is a winner in my book  grab your controller  turn down the lights  and get ready for a spine chilling adventure like no other

 resident evil 3  is a disappointing adaptation of the 1999 original  and a somewhat mediocre follow up to  resident evil 7  and  resident evil 2  remake  but it s a decent game nonetheless  i must admit i really like it  to the point where i replayed it the most out of all resident evil games  the reasons are simple  it s lean  it s smooth  it takes about an afternoon or two to finish  i d call it a comfort game  which is perhaps a bad thing in some ways  i imagine horror is supposed to be anything but comforting  if you keep your expectations in check though  i say there s fun to be had here 
probably one of the best re experiences in my opinion  you ll get the horror and survival of the first two combine with some badass action and cool fights which lets you use weapons you normally would be too greedy to use 
from the moment i loaded the game  i was immediately struck by the stunning graphics and attention to detail  the environments are richly rendered  with crumbling buildings  eerie alleyways  and blood stained streets that create a truly immersive and harrowing atmosphere  the character models are incredibly lifelike  and the facial animations are top notch  conveying the fear and desperation of the protagonists as they navigate the apocalyptic city   one of the standout features of resident evil 3 is the gameplay  the controls are tight and responsive  making movement and combat feel smooth and natural  the addition of the dodge mechanic adds an extra layer of strategy  allowing players to evade attacks and counter with well timed shots  the inventory management system  a staple of the series  adds to the tension as players must carefully manage their resources  balancing ammunition  healing items  and other supplies to survive the relentless onslaught of enemies   speaking of enemies  the creatures in resident evil 3 are some of the most terrifying and challenging foes in the series  the iconic nemesis  a relentless and powerful bioweapon  stalks players throughout the game  providing constant tension and making every encounter a heart pounding battle for survival  the other mutated creatures that populate raccoon city are equally grotesque and formidable  forcing players to strategize and adapt to their unique abilities   the story in resident evil 3 is engaging and well paced  providing a thrilling and intense narrative that keeps players on the edge of their seats  playing as jill valentine  a member of the elite s t a r s  team  players must navigate the city  uncover the truth behind the outbreak  and find a way to escape before it s too late  the story is complemented by well written dialogue  memorable characters  and a sense of urgency that drives the action forward   in addition to the main story  resident evil 3 also includes a multiplayer mode called resident evil resistance  this mode allows players to team up with others online and play as survivors trying to escape from the clutches of a player controlled mastermind who sets traps and controls the undead  while it s a fun addition  it doesn t quite match the intensity and thrill of the main campaign   one area where resident evil 3 falls slightly short is its length  compared to previous entries in the series  the main campaign feels relatively short  clocking in at around 6 8 hours of gameplay  while the game makes up for it with replayability through multiple difficulty levels  unlockable content  and new game plus modes  some players may find themselves craving more content after completing the main story   in conclusion  resident evil 3 is a worthy addition to the iconic franchise  delivering heart pounding survival horror gameplay  stunning visuals  and a gripping story  the intense combat  terrifying enemies  and immersive atmosphere create a truly memorable experience that will keep players on their toes from start to finish  while the main campaign may be relatively short  the overall package is a must play for fans of the series and anyone who loves a good horror game  get ready to face your fears and survive the nightmare of raccoon city in resident evil 3    totally didn t use chatgpt

resident evil 3 is a modern re imagining of the original 1999 resident evil 3  nemesis  the remake now having dropped the somewhat awkward subtitle in favor of just the iterator  having been far too young to play the original game when it came out  i don t have a great frame of reference for how it compares to the original title  the new title however still manages to be an excellent survival shooter with some purposely older mechanics carried over as a deliberate reinvention of the older game s mechanics    what differentiates new resident evil games  7  8  from the older titles and even the remakes are less the obvious shift in player perspective and technical accomplishments  and more the emphasis on trial and error gameplay  playing resident evil 8 i was never in a position where i knew i couldn t possibly beat a boss or encounter  as there would often be an abundance of ammunition and health that affords significant margin for error on the players part  there was never a “correct” combination of weapons and items to beat a boss fight or set piece  if you aimed your shots right and didn t put yourself in a corner to be waled on over and over  the player would probably survive the encounter  in resident evil 3 however  there is a strong emphasis on the one solution the game wants you to use over one the player can organically adapt to if they didn t bring the right supplies to the fight  either through natural attrition or by saving items in crates for later  a midgame forced fight with the nemesis pretty much requires you to have the grenade launcher on you with the appropriate munitions to shoot at walls to stun the nemesis  running out of these  or simply not bringing them will basically make the fight impossible  i spent 20 minutes my first time dumping all my pistol ammo  shotgun shells and grenades into the nemesis and didn t even get to see the second stage of the boss fight  i eventually resorted to the knife  but without stunning the creature  i could only get in one or maybe two measly stabs in before i would have to dodge out of the way  and clearly that fight was a losing one  eventually i just reloaded the checkpoint  equipped the grenade launcher  and won the fight in under 10 minutes  now  it s not like this solution wasn t foreshadowed  the fight arena is littered with mine grenades and explosives ammunition  and the checkpoint before the fight had a document explaining the creation and use of different explosives  so it s not like the game didn t try to warn me what was about to happen  i just wasn t paying attention  but again  this is illustrative of my point of a single “correct” strategy to playing out encounters  resident evil 3 is not at all interested in letting the player find their own ways to beat the game  but slowly refining the playstyle laid out by the rules of the game  even later when i played through the game again with damage bonuses and infinite ammo  boss fights and set piece encounters still felt longer and tougher than using the original playthrough s “correct” playstyle  i m not opposed to this  forcing the player into a specific mindset and playstyle is a fine way to set up a game  and making me use weapons i was keeping in reserve made my gameplay decisions significantly more freeing  because if i concluded that an encounter warranted using those two grenades i d been holding onto  then likely that was the correct moment to use them  enemy encounters in resident evil 3 feel less like a test of my mechanical skill to click on heads  but more a puzzle to solve with the limited inventory i had  sure  i could use my grenade on this high health enemy  but that would mean a hoard of enemies later might take up more ammo than just dumping bullets into this enemy right now  early resident evil games were always an exercise in accounting as much as tactical positioning and player skill to aim properly  and even with a modern coat of paint  this still holds up as a core tenant of the game design  i ve heard that this game is significantly less survival focused than the resident evil 2 remake  and having not played it  i can t vouch for or against that statement  however  i will say that resident evil 3 still managed to make me keep my wits about me when exploring for ammo and healing items more than your average third person shooter  the secondary story in resident evil 3 has the player switching from the perspective of jill valentine  over to carlos  an ignorant umbrella operative who spends most of his time retrieving umbrella secrets and fighting in the margins with jill  you even go back to the police station from resident evil 2  through the timeline is a little fudgy with when you re supposed to be there in relation to leon and claire  the segments with carlos are significantly more action focused  as your starting weapon is an assault rifle with plenty of ammo  and  the player s dodge ability is replaced with a big ol punch that can outright kill some enemies and otherwise stun the larger ones  i can t say i disagree with these segments being in the game  in the initial playthrough at least with jill  the player is always a few missed shots away from a game over  and playing as carlos is a nice change of pace  it makes sense his segments feel more action focused  his thematic place in the narrative is to be prepared to fight off the zombie hoard  it makes sense that he wouldn t be exactly scrounging around for basic pistol ammo and ducking out of the way of every zombie attack   what this does is give you a taste of what the replay experience is going to be like  part of the appeal of replaying resident evil games is the accumulation of power playthrough over playthrough  the first run is a tense survival action game where every shot counts and running from fights is a valid option  the second playthrough is a little less tense since you know where the items are and how to run the level most efficiently  then subsequent playthroughs can be done with the infinite ammo rocket launcher and boosted health  there s even an achievement for beating the game in under two hours to give you an idea of how quickly you can clip through with knowledge of the game and it s solutions  if you ve never played a resident evil game before  but have a basic understanding of “umbrella bad”  then if you re looking for a point to jump in  i d say resident evil 3 isn t a bad place to start  although i hear good things about the remake of the second game  it seems capcom is set on remaking it s older titles for modern audiences and so far the remakes have been knocking it out of the park in balancing the old school game theory while still updating mechanics so it s doesn t feel clunky to play

to overall structure  i have to preface this review with the statement that i never played the original resident evil 3  and probably never will  since i have no0 way or desire to try and launch without original harware    unfortunately  compared to the incredible re2make  r3make is kind of let down  i guess it was released too early after re2make  but this game just feels underwhelming in a all of the living things in the city  the story by itself is fine  the voice acting is great and characters don t do anything stupid  nicholai is a wondertful scun of a villain  and carlos is a great sidekick to jill  it may lack some slower  somber moments of the re2make  but considering that this is a different type of story where the main characters race against the clock it s fine   the follow traditional re formula of searching for keys in an open environment for exploration  the game is mostly linear and broken up into small segments with minimal puzzles and exploration and can be completed in an hour for the first time playthrough and maybe ten minutes if you speedrunning  the exploration that this game offers boils down to tedious backtracking through the same level  there is no interconncted design with shortcuts multiple paths that you can take to avoid danger  only tight corridors and rooms where you have to fight enemies since you can t avoid most of them  the only massive blob like 3rd form that doesn t really make sense in terms of the lore of the series   i personally don t mind these forms but the 2nd form has 2 rehashed boss fights that get old quickly and the reduction of human like  weapon wielding nemesis is overall damaging to the game and the image of nemesis since being able to wield weapons was what made nemesis special and superior to the other  more primitive tyrant variants   the biggest crime of them all is that the iconic  rocket launcher wielding nemesis only appears for a short 3 minute long  on rails section that is easily skippable   during combat nemesis has a wider variety of moves than mr  x and is able to tod combo you like the original but it doesn t matter since the existence of the perfect dodge mechanic and the fact he gets downed with just one grenade makes him a non threat  it s a very low cost to the player and is always beneficial since he drops upgrades and ammo   remake nemesis feels like an absolute joke yet he s so easily fixable by increasing his dynamic appearances  increasing his health to not be downed by a single grenade and lengthening his first form screen time  chalk it up to the dev s design decisions  they chose poorly   in terms of cut content  they cut the first section of the game by making it unexplorable  removed a vast majority of the puzzles and removed one of the most iconic areas in re  the clock tower   this is a sorely missed opportunity to increase the appearances of nemesis and and while they do add some new content  the content cut is much more   now onto the gameplay and level design  the game barely passes as survival horror   meticulously placed key items strewn across a labyrinth like map is how classic re likes to create survival horror by pure design as it forces you to backtrack to areas and continuously encounter enemies you ve already seen before that eat more bullets than you are given   this forces the player to make both a micro and macro decision with each and every enemy encounter in whether to kill them or  risk running past as not killing them will result in the player not only risking themselves presently but also in the future  that is survival horror   the threat in re games are zombies  but for classic re games the threat can be anything  even say guinea pigs and the games will still be survival horror because the games are survival horror by design   this approach to level design is what makes the maps of re so memorable  the placement of every item and enemy is memorable thus making the game as a whole an incredibly memorable experience   re3make only barely passes in doing this in sectioned parts of the game  in the main area of the game for example  the city section  the majority of the area is optional  there s really only one mandatory place in the area that you backtrack to once or twice and you re done with the area which is so unlike classic re and the original since the first main area is usually the most well designed  labyrinth like area that you spend time on for the majority of the playthrough   the areas after are linear with the final 2 areas being the only areas in the game that follow the classic re design philosophy   this is the main cause of the game being so short   in terms of ammo  you get so many you won t know what to do with it   jill is a walking armory and you don t even need to use the plentiful ammo she has since she breaks ankles with her perfect dodge mechanic   you can run past everything with absolute ease due to perfect dodge and it honestly could have easily been fixed if nemesis just appeared more to mess with your rhythm and deplete your resources   the game as a whole leans more towards action horror than survival horror which is kind of fine considering the original started leaning more towards action but the original for the most part was still survival horror  this game is more re4 than re3 though thankfully is not as action heavy as re4 as it s final 2 areas save it s dignity     the massive shift in genre makes sense when you find out the development team for this game is m two  a team primarily consisting of staff members formerly from platinum games which you may or may not know is a company that primarily makes character action games such as bayonetta and metal gear rising   these guys clearly don t know how to make survival horror though the final two areas may say they have some semblance of knowledge  they are too short to say   knowing they re the team behind making the re4 remake is kind of disheartening but re4 isn t survival horror to begin with so they may pull it off   i will give them props though that the set pieces in this game are really cool and jill  carrying and using the railgun against nemesis is in my opinion one of the most badass things in gaming history    what you re paying for when buying this is a 4 hour long game and a multiplayer called re resistance that no one cares for or plays   the game has replayability though much less than other titles in the series so i can t recommend buying it at full price   the game isn t bad  it s fun despite how short it is but it is a bad remake  as stated in my opening sentence the main way it stays true to the original is by being disappointing as it once was   it s weighed down by the design decisions the devs have  h1 overview  h1   b resident evil 3  b  is a remake of the original 1999 game with the same title  however  it is not a 100  accurate recreation of the original  more like a re interpretation  since several mechanics and chapters have been changed significantly compared to the original  re3 takes place in the dreaded raccoon city  where a virus engineered by umbrella corporation got out of control  unleashing death and mayhem upon all  players impersonate stars agent jill valentine  and also umbrella agent carlos in other chapters  living their parallel stories as they try to escape the madness of a doomed city   🟩  b pros  b   list     absolutely exceptional graphics  animations  effects and art direction  raccoon city never looked better     excellent gameplay system that merges together classic resident evil mechanics with modern twists     very good challenge level on hardcore difficulty  with tough and deadly enemies aplenty  efficiency is key     tough bosses  on the hardest setting you will need to save up as much as you can to defeat them     the multiplayer spin off project resistance is included with the game  for those interested    list  🟥  b cons  b   list     ink ribbon saving was completely removed  old school fans of this mechanic may not like this change     the dodge   counter attack mechanic is not always reliable  can get you killed sometimes even if you timed it right    list   h3 rating  excellent  h3   url https   steamcommunity com sharedfiles filedetails  id 2548409603 click here for the complete rating chart  url   https   steamcommunity com sharedfiles filedetails  id 2046577535  on the good side  re3 remake has much to boast  first of all  as already seen with re2 remake  capcom delivered again an amazing quality visually speaking  from highly detailed models to realistic textures  animations and art direction of environments  raccoon city is a sight to behold  this added to the nostalgia of fans is a mix as good as red and green herbs  the gameplay merges well tested mechanic of the series with reworked ones  for example there is a dodge   counter system that works in a way not seen before  while the crafting and combat stay true to the series  usual   challenge level on the hardest setting  hardcore  is definitely solid as even the lowliest enemies will take few hits to kill you  while more dangerous ones can outright 1 shot you at full health  resource conservation and avoiding unnecessary fights become pillars of gameplay  as resources will be needed mostly for the tough boss fights ahead  nemesis proves to be lethal as ever  with both chase sequences and boss encounters against him  each time in a different form   on the bad side instead  the complete removal of ink ribbons even on the hardest difficulty will surely upset the most veteran fans of the original re3  making it an on off option would have been a better compromise  the reworked dodge   counter mechanic is powerful but especially with carlos it proves to be unreliable and will get you damaged or even killed several times  which can be frustrating     b overall re3 remake is an excellent modern transposition of a cult classic  even if it s not an exact 1 1 replica  it still manages to deliver the atmosphere  feeling and satisfying gameplay elements that make the series great  playtime greatly differs by approach  difficulty and skill  it took me 9 hours to complete the game on hardcore  and fully explore every location very accurately  this time may be less  with lower difficulty and or if you choose to be less thorough with item collecting and secondary areas   b    quote liked this review  and want to see more of the same quality  check out  url https   store steampowered com curator 39323490 tamaster s review archive  url  to see all my reviews in a single  convenient location   quote   https   steamcommunity com sharedfiles filedetails  id 2046579446   h1 in depth breakdown  h1  🎮  b gameplay analysis  b  the gameplay is divided into exploration  combat  character management and puzzles    b exploration  b  is done on foot  and in this re compared to other chapters is more linear especially early on  in each chapter there are several areas barred off by some kind of obstacle such as locked doors  chains  fire and so on  finding the right tool to unlock them is essential to progress  being thorough pays off  as each non mandatory area often holds extra ammo  resources and healing items you will surely need later on  especially in boss fights   other than that  there are some collectibles to destroy and secondary puzzles for extra rewards  while exploring  certain areas can spawn new enemies or change their layout based on what happens  so the path you re used to might not always be there later on  however each area has a finite number of enemies  they do not respawn under normal circumstances    b combat  b  in re3 remake is a bit more faster paced than what seen in re2 remake  while normal zombies are as slow as ever  other enemy types and bosses are often fast and deadly  and this is where the dodge   counter mechanic comes in  by pressing space or right click at the right time  it is possible to completely dodge  or deflect  with carlos  an enemy attack  to then counter it in a slow motion fashion that helps you aiming  if done right it can be powerful but the timing feels off and inconsistent  not much reliable especially with carlos    there are various weapons from pistols to grenade launchers  each has a distinct feel and adapts better to specific situations  elite enemies  single enemies  groups of enemies      all of them can be modded if you happen to find the right mods  often hidden behind secondary puzzles  ammo is sometimes of different types  as in the grenade launcher  with different effects  enemies hurt like hell and even the weakest ones will be a serious threat  boss fights have multiple phases and attack patterns  and are very challenging    b character management  b  is done via the inventory panel  which stops time when opened  there you can organize items  most occupy one slot while some two  combining materials to save space and obtain better items  such as ammunition or herb mixtures  is both efficient and recommended in terms of space  safe rooms  scattered throughout levels  offer a safe place to save your game  stash excess items for later in the item box and prepare yourself for the fights ahead  saving is unlimited even on the hardest difficulty  needing no ink  which might upset some fans but is not a big deal in my opinion    b puzzles  b  are not so many compared to other re games  and those present never are overly difficult  mostly puzzles are about collecting a series of items to use in a place  or inputting a sequence in the correct order  many of them are secondary and offer great rewards  such as mods or inventory expansions   https   steamcommunity com sharedfiles filedetails  id 2046585853  🔩  b technical  b   u pc specs  rtx2080ti  ryzen 3900x  32gb ram ddr4  u  there are no stability issues  bugs or other problems to report  the game runs perfectly in 1440p  85hz with maxed out settings  optimization is quite good for the graphics delivered  with fast loading times and no stuttering or popping of any type    h1 final thoughts  h1  re3 remake is overall an excellent reinterpretation of a legendary classic  both on gameplay and visual levels  a recommended purchase for fans of the series for sure  and for horror aficionados in general  it s really damn good    i this game was expectations were high as a big fan of resident evil and capcom s previous game being resident evil 2 remake which has critical success  the question is were my expectations met or did they end up going down the raccoon city drain  lets find out    b  gameplay   b   first up is the gameplay  shooting still feels great as its predecessor shooting system is the exact same over the shoulder style   although it has a lot more action to it  there are still times when i had to limit my ammo and be smart with gunpowder which is keeping it faithful to the original re3 which was more action heavy than its predecessor also   one game changing mechanic is the dodge button  if you successfully time a dodge you are rewarded with max paynesque time slow which will allow you to punish your enemy for trying to chomp on you  admittedly i found myself spamming the button a few times due to being scared out of mind but i manage to actually use the dodge mechanic correctly and pulled off some amazing dodges which made me feel like a badass  its a great mechanic for casual gamers and speedrunners alike    nemesis is also a core part of the game  now nemesis does have a few new tricks up his trench coat  he can jump around like crazy in the streets  tentacle pulls you towards your doom and what seems to be an endless amount of rockets and flamethrower fuel  now because the game runtime is quite short it can appear the nemesis does show up often but in fact  i feel like we did not get to see enough of our tyrant stalker  we only got experience him once or twice in the streets of raccoon and then following types were an arena type boss battle    the boss battles were fun  we only got to experience one boss but in different multiple forms which for me took away from the game as i wanted to see famous bosses like grave digger  i feel like a bit more variety within this particular area would have been great   puzzles  i love resident evil puzzles as its how you figure out to move onto the next area  you have to earn it but in resident evil 3 there are no puzzles  the closest we get is a train segment finding the clear train to go onto  for me this is a big blow as in the original they were plenty of great puzzles to enjoy   within the game you will take control of both carlos and jill  both have different aspects of play  when carlos uses the dodge button perfectly i found him wanting more   the game seems to be missing a lot of content from the original  which i could usually forgive if they replaced it but they didn t  it kinda ruined the pacing of the game and it felt rushed     b  graphics   b   on the pc the game looked great  the characters within the game sometimes looked human in the cutscenes and i would sometimes forget i was playing a game and not watching bears with wheelchairs in which give the game its mature feel    all of the characters within the game were really well made  i completed the game with the standard models and i have to say they were really well done  something that i picked up on that really impressed me was how dirty jill got  no not like that  as you progress through the game jill and carlos both get dirtier  with blood and performances    b  story   b   the game takes places 2 months after the incident at raccoon city  the city is invested with flesh eating zombies and umbrellas terrifying bioweapons  jill is the main protagonist and has several layers to the character with her fighting ptsd and sleeping issues while trying to save the residents of raccoon city which made me full in love with the character more  the story follows jill trying to avoid the umbrella created tyrant nemesis  who had been created with one mission in mind to kill all remaining star members  jill being one of them   the story is the
